*(g) What Step 3 gives and leaves.*
- Gives, given (a)–(b): (S-w) for the groups made at step k+1 whose vertices are marked polymers of kinds (α)–(β) with their touched insertions and vacuum links, old insertions and occurrences away from older layers, under the conditions listed in (13)(d) (κ ≥ κ_D′ := max{κ_vac′, ¼log(9/(2ν)), 3240A/(L−2)}, a new lower bound on κ, accepted by the user, as is L₀ = 5000); the Kotecký–Preiss condition for the reference gas with a° = (ν/32)σ_{k+1} and θ_ref ≤ a°; (f)'s recursion for the constant in k.
- For (R): the Kotecký–Preiss condition and θ_ref ≤ a° are (f)'s.
- Leaves: (13). The amendments (a)–(b) are accepted by the user; the M_Λ pins (L2791) are field-independent and the hull form of (E₁) is V3's Lemma c4-6-env, both checked in (b). The att(k+1, ·) species of the collar rule, the older-layer size and the polylog are proposed in WILSON_IT1_SW_CLOSURE_2026_10_24.md §1–§3 (pl = 2; for review, not reviewed).

### (13) Step 4 of (S): the rate comparison, the root sum for (Z) and the difference count (derived from (12); (a)–(b) reviewed in an earlier form, no fatal finding; (c)–(d) for re-review)

*(a) The rate comparison is one inequality.* With (12)(b)'s extent weight inside (S-w), no comparison of Bałaban's decay e^{−κd_{k+1}} with w_t is needed: the decay in t comes from e^{ν_e·ext_{k+1}(𝒢)} alone. If t(𝒢) = t ≥ 2, then X(𝒢)⁺ is not inside the R_{t−1}^{(k+1)}-neighbourhood of n′ in V3's distance d_T (periodic ℓ∞ distance of the base points plus 1_{i≠j} for the directions, V3 L1196), so some cube of X(𝒢) lies at ℓ∞-distance > C_boxMt − M − 1 step-(k+1) units, at least C_boxt − 2 cube sides, from n′, and ext_{k+1}(𝒢) ≥ C_boxt − 2 (the extent is from every traversal of I(𝒢), n′ among them). Hence
  Σ_{𝒢∋n′, t(𝒢)=t}e^{θ_{k+1}(𝒢)}S_𝒢ℓ_{X(𝒢)} ≤ e^{−ν_e(C_boxt−2)}·Σ_{𝒢∋n′}e^{θ_{k+1} + ν_e·ext_{k+1}}S_𝒢ℓ ≤ e^{2ν_e}C_S′ρ_{k+1}·pl·e^{−(η_V/2)t} ≤ C_Sρ_{k+1}·pl·w_t,
  since ν_eC_box = ν/2 = η_V/2 > η_w = (5/16)η_V and (t+1)³ ≥ 1; C_S := e^{2ν_e}C_S′ with (12)(f)'s C_S′, and t = 1 is the unrestricted sum. This is (S) of (7) (with (12)'s pl), and summed over t′ ≥ t it is (ii-t) for the new groups with W_t at the rate η_w ((5) *New groups*), with the constant C_new = C_gC_S·pl/c₀ in (5)'s (ii-t) (the pl is not absorbed into c₀). The rate per cube side is ν_e = η_V/(2C_box), and C_box ≥ 4A_E is its constraint here ((12)(b) *Base*; the SW route uses C_box ≥ 6, for review); R_t^{(k)} = C_boxM(t+1) with the same C_box at every step ((6)(γ)). Bałaban's rate r and the slots' κ_s are spent entirely on the charges and the entropy ((12)(d), (f)), which is where the counting needs them: a cube of M∖D′ costs log 64 + O(1) against r/648, and the unit of #(G_M), a π_k-cube of the components, costs 81c_DL⁴log2 + 20c_Dr + O(1) against κ_s/4, both independent of t.

*(b) The Kotecký–Preiss root sum for (Z) (D2.3(4)(Z-d); derived).* The c-version of (S-w) ((12)(f)) gives, for the step-(k+1) reference gas, (5.10) with a°(𝒢) = (ν/32)σ_{k+1}(𝒢), so by FV (5.11), for every step-(k+1) traversal b,
  Σ_{Γ∋b}|Φ°(Γ)|e^{a°(Γ)} ≤ Σ_{𝒢∋b}|c_𝒢|e^{2a°(𝒢)} ≤ C_S′ρ_{k+1}·pl =: C_KPρ_{k+1}·pl,
  since 2a° = (ν/16)σ_{k+1} is inside (S-w)'s weight. The weight a°(𝒢) ≥ a_min·n_ins(𝒢) with a_min = ν/(32LM) ((12)(a)). The amplitude is ρ_{k+1}·pl, not τ_k² ((7)(R)).

*(c) The difference count for (Z-e) at the group and cluster levels, and the vacuum tail (derived, in the form (Z-d) needs: one old factor replaced by its T–T′ difference).* For a torus pair T ⊂ T′ of (H3) write ι := ι_{T,k+1}, with "embeds" as in (Z-e). A T–T′ difference of c_𝒢 or Y_𝒢 (embedding 𝒢) has three sources:
- *old vertices and touched insertions:* ΔY_W and Δφ_k(Γ), bounded by the step-k input, the per-position form of (vi)_k ((Z-e) (I), (O) as restated in D2.3(4)) with the full (ii-w) weight, ε^{diff}_k := sup_p[Σ_{W at p}e^{θ_k(W) + ν_rσ_k(W) + ν_e·ext_k(W)}ℓ_{X_W}·sup|ΔY_W| + Σ_{𝒢 at p}e^{(ν/16 + ν_r)σ_k(𝒢) + ν_e·ext_k(𝒢)}ℓ_{X(𝒢)}·sup|Δc_𝒢|] ≤ C_rel^{(k)}e^{−c_kι_{T,k}}ρ_k·pl_k, the sup over the step-k traversals p, which is the inductive clause (Δ := the T–T′ difference at the lifted data, (Z-e); the 𝒢 are the groups made at step k; sup|ΔY_W| is taken over the complex data as (12)(e) takes ε^{old}, (5)'s size O(ρ_k·pl) away from older layers with SW closure §3, the constant in C_rel). At a π_k-cube the sum is not carried, since a per-cube clause carries a factor ~L² ((12)(e)): it is derived afresh at each step from the traversal clause by (12)(e)'s box reduction, with weight e^{θ + ν_rσ + (3/4)ν_e·ext} and constant C(M/ν_e²), which still contains sh + b (extent part (3/8 + 1/L)ν_e·ext; no window spare is needed at cubes), and the new objects at cubes come from (12)(f)'s cube-rooted count. At a traversal the weight contains e^{sh(v)+b(v)} and the window and box spares of (12)(e) up to e^{2c_sh}, and the Γ-vertices (clusters of the step-k reference gas) are bounded by the group part through the cluster level below at step k, whose d carries their per-member constants, so Σ_{v old at p}|Δw̃(v)|e^{b(v)} ≤ K₀ε^{diff}_k, K₀ := (1 + 2e)e^{2c_sh}C_pc′ (C_pc′ the per-piece constant of (12)(e)'s w̃), with Δw̃(v) := e^{sh(v)}·sup|act_T(v) − act_{T′}(ṽ)|, and (12)(e)'s window and box sums run with ε^{diff}_k in place of ε^{old}. A mark on a touched W ∈ 𝔅(M) sits inside the M-vertex: the first-order variation of the touched-insertion product, in (12)(f)'s two-stage count, is ≤ (81#(G_M) + Ln_{k+1}(M))·ε̄^{diff}_k·e^{(#(G_M)+#(M))/8} ≤ (ε̄^{diff}_k/ε_*)b(v)/2 times the vertex's weight (ε̄^{diff}_k := L⁴C(M/ν_e²)K₀ε^{diff}_k, which bounds the sum at a step-(k+1) traversal, LK₀ε^{diff}_k, and at a π_{k+1}-cube, L⁴ derived cube sums, as ε̄^{old} in (12)(e)) — the first term of (12)(f)'s F recursion;
- *the vacuum factors of an M-vertex:* e^{−Ψ°(M)}, δΨ(M) and the links' x_T are sums over all vacuum clusters incompatible with M, including those that do not embed in T. By (Z-c) and (vac)_{k+1} the embedding clusters of T (dependence diameter < ι/4, as in (vac)) and their lifts have the same values at the lifted data; every other cluster of T or T′ incompatible with M (or M̃) has dependence diameter ≥ ι/4, hence support diameter ≥ ι/4 − 2R_D ≥ ι/8 under (TC) (a vacuum cluster's dependence set lies in the R_D-neighbourhood of its support, (Z-g)'s margin, its activity being made of the same localised functions), and #(𝒞) ≥ ι/(8M). So |Δ(vacuum part)| ≤ 3e^{4c#(M)}·tail(M), tail(M) := Σ_{𝒞≁M, #(𝒞)≥ι/(8M)}2·2^s|φ^T(𝒞)|_sup, on T and on T′, ≤ 36c#(M)e^{−ι/(32M)} by (12)(e)'s vacuum sum with its spare e^{#(𝒞)/4}. Put δ_vac := sup_pΣ_{v M-vertex at p}|Δ_vacw̃(v)|e^{b(v)} ≤ C_vacε^{new}e^{−ι/(32M)}, the #(M) absorbed by the second unit of exponent per cube of M reserved in (12)(f) (the first pays (e)'s window decay). The new parts of H̃(M) are exactly local ((Z-c)) and have no difference of their own;
- *the Ursell coefficients* of the clusters: equal on T and T′ for embedding clusters (geometric, (Z-d)).
*Group level.* Telescoping c_𝒢 (or Y_𝒢) over the vertices, each term has one marked vertex: an old vertex, or an M-vertex marked by its vacuum tail or by a touched insertion. Root at the vertex at p as in (12)(f) (the marked-vertex lemma uses its mark hypothesis only in the form Σm·e^{b/2} ≤ (ε_m/ε_*)b/2, which the touched-insertion mark m ∝ b(v)w̃(v) satisfies since be^{b/2} ≤ (2/e)e^{b}), with m := |Δw̃| on the old vertices (ε_m = K₀ε^{diff}_k at a traversal, ε̄^{diff}_k at a cube) and m := |Δ_vacw̃| (or the touched-insertion variation) on the M-vertices (ε_m = δ_vac + (ε̄^{diff}_k/ε_*)·ε^{new}); a group whose difference mark is on an old vertex and which has an M-vertex is counted with the M-mark of (12)(f) as well (two marks with ratios λ, λ′: F₂ ≤ 6λλ′b·e^{3b/4}, which closes the same induction with T ≤ e^{b/2}, F ≤ λbe^{b/2} and be^{−b/4} ≤ 4/e; a bound in b² would not dominate the terms linear in b when b < 1), and one without an M-vertex is M-free, hence quadratic as in (12)(f), the trivial group {W} being the carried insertion's own difference ((6)'s carry of (vi)_k; the dev form at k+1 follows since (4)'s Cauchy step applies to ΔY_W, which vanishes at flat data as both terms do, with b_W^Δ in place of b_W, proportional to sup|ΔY_W|/ᾱ). So, with the (S-w) weight at every step-(k+1) position, cubes included (the new groups at cubes from (12)(f)'s cube-rooted count, with the marks' cube sums ε̄^{diff}_k),
  δ_{k+1} := sup_pΣ_{𝒢 at p}e^{(ν/16 + ν_r)σ_{k+1}(𝒢) + ν_e·ext_{k+1}(𝒢)}ℓ_{X(𝒢)}|Δc_𝒢| ≤ K_Δ[(ε^{new} + ε̄^{old} + ε̄^{ref,k})/ε_*]·ε̄^{diff}_k + 2C_winδ_vac,
and the same for the new insertions' differences ΔY_W with (ii-w)'s weight; these two parts at the step-(k+1) traversals with the carried part ((6); per traversal L^{−1}(ρ̃_k/ρ̃_{k+1}) ≤ L^{−1}(1+β₀)⁸ of ε^{diff}_k, as in (12)(f); (12)(b) *It carries* uses only weights, positions and normalisations, an old W keeping σ, ext and θ, and a carried W's sup|ΔY_W| at step k+1 is its step-k value, the agreement radius ι_{T,k+1}/4 step-(k+1) units being ι_{T,k}/4 step-k units, (Z-e)(c)) make ε^{diff}_{k+1}, whose cube sums are derived as above and whose Γ-vertices at step k+2 are bounded through the cluster level below. With ε^{new} + ε̄^{old} + ε̄^{ref,k} ≤ C″C_S′ρ_{k+1}·pl_{k+1} ((12)(e)–(f); C″ depends on L, M, ν_e, β₀ and C_win, C_M through the box factor L⁴C(M/ν_e²)) and ε^{new} ≤ C″C_S′ρ_{k+1}·pl_{k+1}:
  ε^{diff}_{k+1} ≤ [L^{−1}(1+β₀)⁸ + 2L⁴C(M/ν_e²)K₀K_ΔC″C_S′ρ_{k+1}pl_{k+1}/ε_*]·ε^{diff}_k + 4C_winC_vacC″C_S′ρ_{k+1}pl_{k+1}·e^{−ι/(32M)},
so with c_k := c := min(c₁, 1/(32M)) for every k, ι_{T,k} = Lι_{T,k+1}, ρ_k·pl_k ≤ (1+β₀)⁷ρ_{k+1}·pl_{k+1} ((12)(f)), L^{−1}(1+β₀)^{15} ≤ 1/8 ((12)(f)) and the second term in the bracket ≤ (1+β₀)^{−7}/8 for g_K small, and with ε^{diff}_k, the derived cube sums and (O)_k at every position all bounded by C_rel^{(k)}e^{−cι_{T,k}}ρ_k·pl_k (the inductive statement): put C_rel^{(k+1)} := ¼e^{−c(L−1)ι_{T,k+1}}C_rel^{(k)} + C⁎, C⁎ := 4C_winC_vacC″C_S′. Then ε^{diff}_{k+1} ≤ C_rel^{(k+1)}e^{−cι_{T,k+1}}ρ_{k+1}·pl_{k+1}, and, since the bound on δ_{k+1} above does not depend on the position, δ_{k+1} ≤ C_rel^{(k+1)}e^{−cι_{T,k+1}}ρ_{k+1}·pl_{k+1} at every step-(k+1) position, cubes included; hence C_rel^{(k)} ≤ max(C_rel^{(1)}, 2C⁎) uniformly in k and in the torus (the factor ρ_{k+1}·pl gives the contraction). The sup form here is the inductive clause; it implies (Z-e)'s form relative to D(k)(ii)'s right side, so (5)'s conversion constant is applied once, not iterated. In the telescoped products each unmarked factor is bounded by |c^T| + |c^{T′}| (or the same for w̃), which doubles the rooted sums: (12)(e)'s condition and the KP check below hold with the factor 2 for g_K small.
*Cluster level.* For a cluster Γ of the step-(k+1) reference gas, Φ°(Γ) = (Ursell coefficient)·∏_{𝒢∈Γ}c_𝒢, the coefficient depending only on the incompatibility graph, the same on T and T′ for embedding Γ. So ΔΦ°(Γ) telescopes over the groups of Γ, and the marked-vertex lemma of (12)(f) applies with vertices := groups, links := incompatibility, FV Thm 5.4's split a := a°, d(𝒢) := ν_rσ_{k+1}(𝒢) + ν_e·ext_{k+1}(𝒢) + 2c_sh + 1 + log C_pc′ (d := 0 for the unweighted form; the log C_pc′ pays the per-member factor of a Γ-vertex's C_v at step k+2, (12)(e)), weights |c_𝒢|e^{a°(𝒢)+d(𝒢)}, charges a°(𝒢) = (ν/32)σ_{k+1}(𝒢), the threshold δ_* := ν/(64·81M) (positions of 𝒢 ≤ 81Mσ_{k+1}(𝒢)) and the Kotecký–Preiss check Σ_{𝒢′≁𝒢}|c_𝒢′|e^{2a°+d} ≤ 81Mσ_{k+1}(𝒢)·e^{2c_sh+1}C_pc′C_S′ρ_{k+1}pl ≤ a°(𝒢)/2 for g_K small, on T and on T′, which uses exactly (12)(f)'s weight e^{(ν/16 + ν_r)σ + ν_e·ext}ℓ_X with ℓ_X ≥ 1 ((4), l.1320) (zero margin in σ); FV Thm 5.4's tree-graph bound on the Ursell coefficients is multilinear in the weights, so the marked version holds with δ^d_{k+1} := sup_pΣ_{𝒢 at p}|Δc_𝒢|e^{2a°+d} ≤ e^{2c_sh+1}C_pc′δ_{k+1} and F_g(𝒢) ≤ (2δ^d_{k+1}/δ_*)a°(𝒢)e^{a°(𝒢)/2}, and, with d(Γ) := Σ_{𝒢∈Γ}d(𝒢), at every position p,
  Σ_{Γ at p, embeds}|Φ°_T(Γ) − Φ°_{T′}(Γ̃)|e^{d(Γ)} ≤ Σ_{𝒢 at p}[|Δc_𝒢|e^{a°+d}T_g(𝒢) + |c_𝒢|e^{a°+d}F_g(𝒢)] ≤ δ^d_{k+1} + (2δ^d_{k+1}/δ_*)Σ_{𝒢 at p}|c_𝒢|e^{2a°+d} ≤ δ^d_{k+1}(1 + 2e^{2c_sh+1}C_pc′C_S′ρ_{k+1}pl/δ_*) ≤ 2δ^d_{k+1} ≤ 2e^{2c_sh+1}C_pc′δ_{k+1}
for g_K small; with d := 0 the same gives Σ_{Γ∋b, embeds}|ΔΦ°(Γ)| ≤ 2δ_{k+1} ≤ 2C_rel^{(k+1)}e^{−cι_{T,k+1}}ρ_{k+1}·pl (δ_{k+1}'s bound at every position, above). The d-weighted form bounds the Γ-vertices of ε^{diff}_{k+1}, with their shares, charges and C_v, by 2e^{2c_sh+1}C_pc′ times its group part (the term 2e·e^{2c_sh}C_pc′ of K₀), which closes the recursion above; the unweighted form is the input of (Z-d), with G := 2 and the amplitude ρ_{k+1}·pl; with the non-embedding clusters bounded by the a°-weight (D2.3(4)(Z-f)), C_Z^{(k+1)} = 2C_S′ + 2C_rel^{(k+1)} at the rate c^Z = min(c, ν/(512M)) = min(c₁, ν/(512M)) ((T) is not used). *Used, not derived here:* the step-1 base of ε^{diff} in the per-position weighted form (D2.3(4)(Z-h), derived from V4 with one structural input for the user's confirmation, the weights absorbed by e^{4η𝔰} as in (12)(b) *Base*); (12)(b) *It carries* for the carried part; the sub-domain form of D(k)(iii)-dev (SW closure §3, for review) for the old factors at complex backgrounds; (vac)_{k+1} and (Z-c) as derived in D2.3(4); the margin of "embeds" (R_D ≤ 6L²MR_k, (Z-g)).

*(d) What (S) is.* Given the amendments (12)(a)–(b) (accepted by the user) and (R-m′) of (12)(f) (accepted by the user provisionally, (12)(f)), under κ ≥ κ_D′, L ≥ L₀ = 5000 (accepted by the user), 8(1+β₀)^{15} ≤ L (β₀ ≤ 1/2, accepted by the user; CRE (2.6) allows it for g small, D2.2(6)(f) *L.*), C_box ≥ 4A_E, κ_s ≥ κ_s^{(12)} (after κ, L; before M; raised by ν_e by (12)(f)'s (T2), accepted by the user), M ≥ 100, c_πα_{1,k+1}·4096M ≤ ν ((12)(d)'s transports) and c_πα_{1,k+1}M ≤ ν/64 ((8), for σ = # + n/M), r/648 ≥ 8 ((12)(f)), the conditions of (10)–(11) (972A ≤ δλ_Lκ, R-L3's list, 20c_πM‖a′‖_∞ ≤ 1 on the arcs of (4)(e)), ν ≤ 1/1000, R-m with (R-m′), the torus condition (TC) of D2.3(4) for the vacuum tail, w_t at the rate η_w = (5/16)η_V (with ν_e := ν/(2C_box); D2.6 closure §6.5), and g_K small, and given the inputs of (11)(g) and (12)(g) (D2.6's kinds (γ) and species, the older-layer complex-data size, the polylog of (11)(g), absorbed in pl = 1 + (log g_k^{−2})^{(q₁−q₀)₊}, D(k)(iii)'s dev clause for the touched insertions): (S) of (7) holds in the form (S-w) uniformly in k ≤ K ((12)(f)), with (a)'s rate comparison (C_S := e^{2ν_e}C_S′; the entropy per π_k-cube of the components, the unit of #(G_M), is 81c_DL⁴log2 + 20c_Dr + O(1) against κ_s/4, and per cube of M∖D′ log 64 + O(1) against r/648), and (ii-t) for the new groups with the constant C_new = C_gC_S·pl/c₀ in (5).
